## Clinical trials (results posted, new Phase 3, terminations, status changes)
- **LLY** · **RESULTS POSTED** · [NCT05433584](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT05433584) P4 · ACTIVE_NOT_RECRUITING · primary completion 2025-10-21 — A Study of Tirzepatide Compared With Intensified Conventional Care in Adult Participants With Type 2 Diabetes (Type 2 Diabetes)
- **LLY** · **RESULTS POSTED** · [NCT06220669](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT06220669) P2 · TERMINATED · primary completion 2025-10-18 — A Study of LY3541860 in Adult Participants With Relapsing Multiple Sclerosis (Multiple Sclerosis)
- **NVO** · **NEW PHASE 3** · [NCT07931547](https://clinicaltrials.gov/study/NCT07931547) P3 · NOT_YET_RECRUITING · primary completion 2028-07-31 — A Research Study Investigating How Well Zenagamtide Lowers Blood Sugar and Body Weight Compared to Placebo in People With Type 2 Diabetes and Chronic Kidney Disease Treated With Once Daily Basal Insulin Alone or in Combination With Metformin, SGLT2i, or Both (Type 2 Diabetes, Chronic Kidney Disease)
